```turtle
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:engineOutputFormulaFactorF3 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:engineOutputFormulaFactorF4 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloW
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Maximum continuous rating power must be no less than f1*f2*f3*(f4*Delta+P0), within a relative tolerance of 1e-6, and must be at least 740 kW for ice classes IB and IC." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?ice ;
                      nltl:engineOutputFormulaBasePowerP0 ?p0Node ;
                      nltl:engineOutputFormulaDisplacement ?deltaNode ;
                      nltl:engineOutputFormulaFactorF1 ?f1Node ;
                      nltl:engineOutputFormulaFactorF2 ?f2Node ;
                      nltl:engineOutputFormulaFactorF3 ?f3Node ;
                      nltl:engineOutputFormulaFactorF4 ?f4Node ;
                      nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?actualNode .

                ?p0Node qudt:numericValue ?p0 .
                ?deltaNode qudt:numericValue ?delta .
                ?f1Node qudt:numericValue ?f1 .
                ?f2Node qudt:numericValue ?f2 .
                ?f3Node qudt:numericValue ?f3 .
                ?f4Node qudt:numericValue ?f4 .
                ?actualNode qudt:numericValue ?actual .

                BIND ((?f1 * ?f2 * ?f3 * ((?f4 * ?delta) + ?p0)) AS ?expected)
                BIND (IF(ABS(?expected) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                         ABS(?expected) * 0.000001,
                         0.000000001) AS ?tolerance)

                FILTER (
                    (?actual + ?tolerance < ?expected)
                    ||
                    (
                        ?ice IN (nltl:iceClassIb, nltl:iceClassIc)
                        && ?actual < 740
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

For linear calculation methods: web plates and flange elements in compression and shear are to fulfil the relevant buckling criteria specified by the Classification Society; nominal shear stress in member web plates is to be less than sigma_y/sqrt(3); and nominal von Mises stress in member flanges is to be less than 1.15*sigma_y.

        [

```turtle
                                    ]
                                ]
                            ]
                        ]
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMemberWeb ;
                        sh:class nltl:structuralMemberWeb ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:nominalShearStress ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:node [
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ] ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;

                                    ]

                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ]

            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?calculationCase .
                ?calculationCase nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberWeb ?web .
                ?web nltl:nominalShearStress ?shearQuantity .
                ?shearQuantity qudt:numericValue ?shearValue .
                $this nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldQuantity .
                ?yieldQuantity qudt:numericValue ?yieldValue .
                FILTER (
                    ?shearValue >=
                    (?yieldValue / 1.7320508075688772)
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Nominal flange von Mises stress in each structural-member flange must be less than 1.15 times yield strength." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?calculationCase .
                ?calculationCase nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberFlange ?flange .
                ?flange nltl:nominalFlangeVonMisesStress ?stressQuantity .
                ?stressQuantity qudt:numericValue ?stressValue .
                $this nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldQuantity .
                ?yieldQuantity qudt:numericValue ?yieldValue .
                FILTER (
                    ?stressValue >=
                    (1.15 * ?yieldValue)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

nltl:I2_064_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCalculationCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:calculationCase ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:calculationMethod ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue
                    nltl:nonlinearCalculationMethodValue
                    nltl:prescribedAnalyticalProcedure
                    nltl:directCalculationMethodValue
                )
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each calculation case must identify one permitted calculation method."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A linear calculation case requires a ship yield-strength quantity with a decimal value in MPa." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:yieldStrength ?yield .
                    ?yield qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                           qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> .
                    FILTER (datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A linear calculation case requires each linked structural member to have a typed flange and web." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case ;
                      nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?member a nltl:structuralMember . }
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberFlange ?flange .
                    ?flange a nltl:structuralMemberFlange .
                }
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberWeb ?web .
                    ?web a nltl:structuralMemberWeb .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each applicable structural member must provide buckling-criteria satisfaction evidence." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case ;
                      nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?member nltl:bucklingCriteriaSatisfied true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each applicable web must provide nominal shear stress as a decimal MPa quantity." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case ;
                      nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberWeb ?web .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?web nltl:nominalShearStress ?stress .
                    ?stress qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                            qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> .
                    FILTER (datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each applicable flange must provide nominal von Mises stress as a decimal MPa quantity." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case ;
                      nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberFlange ?flange .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?flange nltl:nominalFlangeVonMisesStress ?stress .
                    ?stress qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                            qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> .
                    FILTER (datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Nominal shear stress in each applicable web must be less than yield strength divided by sqrt(3)." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?yield ;
                      nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                ?yield qudt:numericValue ?yieldValue .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberWeb ?web .
                ?web nltl:nominalShearStress ?stress .
                ?stress qudt:numericValue ?stressValue .
                FILTER (datatype(?yieldValue) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (datatype(?stressValue) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (?stressValue >= (?yieldValue / 1.7320508075688772))
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Nominal flange von Mises stress must be less than 1.15 times yield strength." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCalculationCase ?case ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?yield ;
                      nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?case nltl:calculationMethod nltl:linearCalculationMethodValue .
                ?yield qudt:numericValue ?yieldValue .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberFlange ?flange .
                ?flange nltl:nominalFlangeVonMisesStress ?stress .
                ?stress qudt:numericValue ?stressValue .
                FILTER (datatype(?yieldValue) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (datatype(?stressValue) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (?stressValue >= (1.15 * ?yieldValue))
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Buckling criteria must be confirmed for the structural member."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMemberWeb ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:structuralMemberWeb ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:nominalShearStress ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMemberFlange ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:structuralMemberFlange ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:nominalFlangeVonMisesStress ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Nominal shear stress in each structural member web must be less than yield strength divided by sqrt(3)." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldQuantity .
                ?yieldQuantity qudt:numericValue ?yieldValue .
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberWeb ?web .
                ?web nltl:nominalShearStress ?shearQuantity .
                ?shearQuantity qudt:numericValue ?shearValue .
                FILTER (?shearValue >= (?yieldValue / 1.7320508075688772))
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Nominal von Mises stress in each structural member flange must be less than 1.15 times yield strength." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldQuantity .
                ?yieldQuantity qudt:numericValue ?yieldValue .
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:hasStructuralMemberFlange ?flange .
                ?flange nltl:nominalFlangeVonMisesStress ?stressQuantity .
                ?stressQuantity qudt:numericValue ?stressValue .
                FILTER (?stressValue >= (1.15 * ?yieldValue))
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
For ships operating in areas, and during periods, where ice accretion is likely to occur, means to prevent the accumulation of ice on antennas required for navigation and communication shall be provided.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_accretion_likely = true, THEN every required navigation or communication antenna shall have ice_accumulation_prevention_means_present = true.
Required inputs: Antenna inventory, ice-accretion applicability, and prevention arrangement

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO26_009_ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:message "iceAccretionLikely must be explicitly recorded as false when the prevention requirement is not applicable."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "iceAccretionLikely must be explicitly recorded as true when the prevention requirement is applicable."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasRequiredNavigationOrCommunicationAntenna ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:antennaIceAccumulationPreventionPresent ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue true ;
                        sh:message "Every required navigation or communication antenna must have ice accumulation prevention means."
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "When ice accretion is likely, at least one required navigation or communication antenna must be identified."
            ]
        }
    ) .
```

Original source text:
(iv) In other ice-strengthened areas, the ice load parameters (Pavg, bNonBow and wNonBow) are determined independently of the hull shape and based on a fixed load patch aspect ratio, AR = 3.6. (v) Design ice forces calculated according to I2.3.2.1 (iii) are applicable for bow forms where the buttock angle gamma at the stem is positive and less than 80 deg, and the normal frame angle beta' at the centre of the foremost sub-region, as defined in I2.3.2.1 (i), is greater than 10 deg.

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix nltlg: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

nltlg:QuantityValueShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A quantity value must contain one decimal numeric value."
    ] .

nltlg:AverageIcePressureShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:node nltlg:QuantityValueShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Average ice pressure must use MPa."
    ] .

nltlg:AngleShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:node nltlg:QuantityValueShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The angle must use degrees."
    ] .

nltlg:AspectRatioShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:node nltlg:QuantityValueShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The load patch aspect ratio must be dimensionless."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minInclusive 3.6 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 3.6 ;
        sh:message "The fixed load patch aspect ratio must be 3.6."
    ] .

nltlg:LengthShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:node nltlg:QuantityValueShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The length must use metres."
    ] .

nltlg:HullAreaAssignmentShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:hullAreaAssignment ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:assignedHullArea ;
        sh:class nltl:hullAreaValue ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:otherIceStrengthenedArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The assignment must identify the other ice-strengthened area."
    ] .

nltlg:ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasHullAreaAssignment ;
        sh:node nltlg:HullAreaAssignmentShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide a hull-area assignment for the other ice-strengthened area."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullArea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide its hull area."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:averageIcePressure ;
        sh:node nltlg:AverageIcePressureShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide its average ice pressure."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadPatchAspectRatio ;
        sh:node nltlg:AspectRatioShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must use the required fixed load patch aspect ratio."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:nonBowLoadPatchHeight ;
        sh:node nltlg:LengthShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide the non-bow load patch height."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:nonBowLoadPatchLength ;
        sh:node nltlg:LengthShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide the non-bow load patch length."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:buttockAngle ;
        sh:node nltlg:AngleShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide the buttock angle at the stem."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:normalFrameAngle ;
        sh:node nltlg:AngleShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide the normal frame angle."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowFormApplicability ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Bow-form applicability must be explicitly recorded."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Bow-form applicability must be true exactly when the buttock angle is greater than 0 and less than 80 degrees and the normal frame angle is greater than 10 degrees." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bowFormApplicability ?app .
                $this nltl:buttockAngle ?gammaNode .
                $this nltl:normalFrameAngle ?betaNode .
                ?gammaNode qudt:numericValue ?gamma .
                ?betaNode qudt:numericValue ?beta .
                FILTER (
                    (?app = true &&
                     ! (?gamma > 0 && ?gamma < 80 && ?beta > 10))
                    ||
                    (?app = false &&
                     (?gamma > 0 && ?gamma < 80 && ?beta > 10))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF009ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide exactly one ice class for applicability assessment."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "An IA Super or IA ship must provide exactly one construction stage date for applicability assessment." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT ?this
            WHERE {
                ?this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass .
                FILTER (
                    ?iceClass IN (
                        nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                        nltl:iceClassIa
                    )
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?this nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The conditional requirement is applicable only to IA Super or IA ships constructed before 1 September 2003." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT ?this
                    WHERE {
                        ?this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                              nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .
                        FILTER (
                            ?iceClass IN (
                                nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                                nltl:iceClassIa
                            )
                        )
                        FILTER (
                            YEAR(?constructionDate) < 2003 ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?constructionDate) = 2003 &&
                                (
                                    MONTH(?constructionDate) < 9 ||
                                    (
                                        MONTH(?constructionDate) = 9 &&
                                        DAY(?constructionDate) < 1
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "An applicable ship must identify compliance with section 3.2.2 or 3.2.4 of the Ice Class Regulations of 2017." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT ?this
                    WHERE {
                        ?this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                              nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .
                        FILTER (
                            !(
                                ?iceClass IN (
                                    nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                                    nltl:iceClassIa
                                )
                                &&
                                (
                                    YEAR(?constructionDate) < 2003 ||
                                    (
                                        YEAR(?constructionDate) = 2003 &&
                                        (
                                            MONTH(?constructionDate) < 9 ||
                                            (
                                                MONTH(?constructionDate) = 9 &&
                                                DAY(?constructionDate) < 1
                                            )
                                        )
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "An applicable ship must provide exactly one construction stage date for applicability assessment."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:deliveryDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "An applicable ship must provide exactly one delivery date for the twenty-year deadline calculation."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:assessmentDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "An applicable ship must provide exactly one assessment date for deadline evaluation."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethod ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethodValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point2Method
                    nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point4Method
                ) ;
                sh:message "The compliance method must be section 3.2.2 or section 3.2.4 of the Ice Class Regulations of 2017."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The required compliance must be achieved no later than 1 January in the year twenty years after delivery." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT ?this
                    WHERE {
                        ?this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                              nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate ;
                              nltl:deliveryDate ?deliveryDate ;
                              nltl:assessmentDate ?assessmentDate .
                        FILTER (
                            ?iceClass IN (
                                nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                                nltl:iceClassIa
                            )
                        )
                        FILTER (
                            YEAR(?constructionDate) < 2003 ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?constructionDate) = 2003 &&
                                (
                                    MONTH(?constructionDate) < 9 ||
                                    (
                                        MONTH(?constructionDate) = 9 &&
                                        DAY(?constructionDate) < 1
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                        FILTER (
                            YEAR(?assessmentDate) > YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20 ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?assessmentDate) = YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20 &&
                                (
                                    MONTH(?assessmentDate) > 1 ||
                                    (
                                        MONTH(?assessmentDate) = 1 &&
                                        DAY(?assessmentDate) > 1
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF009_ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        ) ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide a canonical Finnish-Swedish ice-class value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide its construction-stage date as an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:deliveryDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:message "The delivery date must be an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:assessmentDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:message "The assessment date must be an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an IA or IA Super ship constructed before 1 September 2003, the applicable 2017 Ice Class Regulation method, delivery date, assessment date, and twenty-year deadline evidence must conform to TRF-009." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?ice ;
                      nltl:constructionStageDate ?stage .

                FILTER (
                    ?ice IN (
                        nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                        nltl:iceClassIa
                    )
                )
                FILTER (datatype(?stage) = xsd:date)
                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?stage) < 2003 ||
                    (
                        YEAR(?stage) = 2003 &&
                        (
                            MONTH(?stage) < 9 ||
                            (MONTH(?stage) = 9 && DAY(?stage) < 1)
                        )
                    )
                )

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:deliveryDate ?delivery .
                        FILTER (datatype(?delivery) = xsd:date)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:assessmentDate ?assessment .
                        FILTER (datatype(?assessment) = xsd:date)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethod ?method .
                        FILTER (
                            ?method IN (
                                nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point2Method,
                                nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point4Method
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:deliveryDate ?delivery ;
                              nltl:assessmentDate ?assessment .
                        FILTER (datatype(?delivery) = xsd:date)
                        FILTER (datatype(?assessment) = xsd:date)
                        FILTER (
                            YEAR(?assessment) > YEAR(?delivery) + 20 ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?assessment) = YEAR(?delivery) + 20 &&
                                (
                                    MONTH(?assessment) > 1 ||
                                    (MONTH(?assessment) = 1 && DAY(?assessment) > 1)
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:TRF009ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide an ice class to determine applicability of TRF-009."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide its construction stage date to determine applicability of TRF-009."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:TRF009ApplicableShape
        gen:TRF009NotApplicableShape
    ) .

gen:TRF009ApplicableShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:deliveryDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable ship must provide its delivery date for the TRF-009 deadline."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:assessmentDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable ship must provide an assessment date for the TRF-009 deadline."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethod ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point2Method
            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point4Method
        ) ;
        sh:message "An applicable ship must identify compliance with section 3.2.2 or 3.2.4 of the 2017 Ice Class Regulations."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "TRF-009 applies to an IA Super or IA ship constructed before 1 September 2003, and the ship must not be assessed before 1 January of the year twenty years after delivery." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?ice ;
                       nltl:constructionStageDate ?construction ;
                       nltl:deliveryDate ?delivery ;
                       nltl:assessmentDate ?assessment .
                FILTER (
                    ?ice IN (
                        nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                        nltl:iceClassIa
                    )
                )
                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?construction) < 2003 ||
                    (
                        YEAR(?construction) = 2003 &&
                        (
                            MONTH(?construction) < 9 ||
                            (
                                MONTH(?construction) = 9 &&
                                DAY(?construction) < 1
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )
                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?assessment) > YEAR(?delivery) + 20 ||
                    (
                        YEAR(?assessment) = YEAR(?delivery) + 20 &&
                        (
                            MONTH(?assessment) > 1 ||
                            (
                                MONTH(?assessment) = 1 &&
                                DAY(?assessment) >= 1
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF009NotApplicableShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The ship is neither IA Super or IA constructed before 1 September 2003, so the conditional TRF-009 obligation is not applicable." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?ice ;
                       nltl:constructionStageDate ?construction .
                FILTER (
                    NOT (
                        ?ice IN (
                            nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                            nltl:iceClassIa
                        )
                        &&
                        (
                            YEAR(?construction) < 2003 ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?construction) = 2003 &&
                                (
                                    MONTH(?construction) < 9 ||
                                    (
                                        MONTH(?construction) = 9 &&
                                        DAY(?construction) < 1
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
Where fixed water-based firefighting systems are located in a space separate from the main fire pumps and use their own independent sea suction, this sea suction shall also be capable of being cleared of ice accumulation.
Normalized requirement:
IF fixed_water_system_separate_from_main_fire_pumps = true AND independent_sea_suction_used = true, THEN sea_suction_ice_clearing_capability = true.
Required inputs: System layout, suction configuration, and ice-clearing arrangement

```turtle
    a sh:NodeShape ;

        sh:path nltl:fixedWaterSystemSeparateFromMainFirePumps ;

        sh:path nltl:independentSeaSuctionUsed ;

        sh:path nltl:seaSuctionIceClearingCapability ;
```

```turtle
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO_060_ApplicableBranch
        gen:IMO_060_SeparateSystemNotApplicableBranch
        gen:IMO_060_IndependentSuctionNotApplicableBranch
    ) .

gen:IMO_060_ApplicableBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:fixedWaterSystemSeparateFromMainFirePumps ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:independentSeaSuctionUsed ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:seaSuctionIceClearingCapability ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "When the separate fixed water system uses an independent sea suction, the sea suction must be capable of being cleared of ice accumulation."
    ] .

gen:IMO_060_SeparateSystemNotApplicableBranch

        sh:path nltl:fixedWaterSystemSeparateFromMainFirePumps ;
        sh:hasValue false ;

        sh:path nltl:fixedWaterSystemSeparateFromMainFirePumps ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;

        sh:hasValue false ;
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-080-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For applicable propeller/ice interaction components linked to the ship, effectiveIceLoadCycleCount must equal iceLoadCycleCount multiplied by propellerBladeCount." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasComponent ?component .
                ?component a nltl:propellerIceInteractionComponent ;
                           nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ?cycles ;
                           nltl:propellerBladeCount ?blades ;
                           nltl:effectiveIceLoadCycleCount ?quantity .
                ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?effective .
                FILTER (
                    ?effective !=
                    (xsd:decimal(?cycles) * xsd:decimal(?blades))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-080-PropellerIceInteractionComponentShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:propellerIceInteractionComponent ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable propeller/ice interaction component must provide exactly one ice load cycle count."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable propeller/ice interaction component must provide exactly one propeller blade count."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:effectiveIceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "An applicable propeller/ice interaction component must provide exactly one unitless effective ice load cycle count."
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:TRF_080_StaticCalculationShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "iceLoadCycleCount is required as a single integer input."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "propellerBladeCount is required as a single integer input."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:effectiveIceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "effectiveIceLoadCycleCount is required as a unitless quantity value."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "effectiveIceLoadCycleCount must equal iceLoadCycleCount multiplied by propellerBladeCount within a relative tolerance of 1e-6, with an absolute tolerance of 1e-6 near zero." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ?iceCycles ;
                      nltl:propellerBladeCount ?bladeCount ;
                      nltl:effectiveIceLoadCycleCount ?resultNode .
                ?resultNode qudt:numericValue ?actual .

                BIND ((?iceCycles * ?bladeCount) AS ?expected)
                BIND (
                    IF(
                        ABS(?expected) < 0.001,
                        0.000001,
                        0.000001 * ABS(?expected)
                    )
                    AS ?tolerance
                )
                FILTER (ABS(?actual - ?expected) > ?tolerance)
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-080> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "iceLoadCycleCount is required as a single integer value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "propellerBladeCount is required as a single integer value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:effectiveIceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "effectiveIceLoadCycleCount is required as a single unitless quantity value."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "effectiveIceLoadCycleCount must equal iceLoadCycleCount multiplied by propellerBladeCount within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 and an absolute fallback of 1e-7." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ?nice ;
                      nltl:propellerBladeCount ?blades ;
                      nltl:effectiveIceLoadCycleCount ?quantity .
                ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?actual ;
                          qudt:unit qudt:UNITLESS .
                BIND ((?nice * ?blades) AS ?expected)
                FILTER (
                    ABS(?actual - ?expected) >
                    ((0.000001 * ABS(?expected)) + 0.0000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?
Record grades, reviewer identity, evidence, and notes in the workbook. Do not infer full-requirement correctness from these two cases.
